HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001","root_001040/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_045918356734f2a8425a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:5:1\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"96:5:5\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Öğretmek, bilinmeyeni ayırt edilebilir hâle getiren işaretler kurar; bu yüzden öğrenen kişi ibadeti engellemekle doğru sınır koymayı ayırt etmekten sorumludur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1cd549f70c0aba4fbd49","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:5:2\",\"root_000059\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İnsana kazandırılan algı, doğru öz yargıyı garanti etmez; uyanmış farkındalık yeniden kişinin kendisine kıvrılıp yanılgıya dönüşebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002","root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3bbd2cac605cecad60e2","text":"1. Teaching is a transfer from non-knowledge into marked awareness. Evidence: `ع ل م B001` knowing/teaching and `ع ل م B002` distinguishing sign; `ء ن س B002` perceiving into awareness by sight/sense/hearing. Structural cue: `عَلَّمَ` surrounds `ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ` with the negative `مَا لَمْ يَعْلَمْ`, making human knowledge received rather than possessed. Mechanism: what was not in awareness becomes knowable through teaching and marks. Abductive move: the packet supplies the teaching verb and sign branches; I infer that knowledge is not innate self-sufficiency but a marked gift. Reading change: the human is defined by received instruction before being judged for arrogance. 2. The ayah activates a mirror with 96:14, where the question is whether he knows God sees. Evidence: 96:5 `ع ل م B001` teaching what he did not know; 96:14 `ع ل م B001` and `ر ء ي B001/B012` seeing/showing. Structural cue: the same root `ع ل م` recurs at the pivot of rebuke. Mechanism: early bestowed knowledge creates responsibility; later ignorance is no longer lack but refusal to account for being seen. Abductive move: the packet gives repeated `علم` and divine seeing; I infer an accountability loop from taught knowledge to moral knowing. Reading change: “taught what he did not know” becomes the basis for “does he not know?”, a charge rather than a neutral gift.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_63f88e409224473333f1","text":"3. **A perceiver whose awareness can still curve back into error.** `ء ن س:B002` supplies perceiving or bringing something into awareness. The fixed `الإنسان` stands between taught knowledge and the reflexive `رآه` of 96:7; the same human then becomes the subject of boundary-crossing in 96:6. The perception branch makes the human an awareness-bearing recipient, while the sequence distinguishes awareness from accurate self-judgment. I infer that the taught capacity can be redirected onto a false estimate of the self. The line thus makes later transgression an abuse of awakened perception rather than simple unawareness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_69cf5251a820f6fdfcd6","text":"96:14 retrospectively reclassified the whole teaching cluster: knowledge culminates not in information but in knowing that Allah sees. The surprise is that the primary missing knowledge is relational accountability, not merely literacy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_734c658d46d1259ea4e9","text":"96:7 was the strongest later activation: `استغنى` made the causative/simple contrast in `علّم ... لم يعلم` an internal refutation of independence. 96:9–10 then made the distinguishing-mark branch `ع ل م:B002` ethically consequential, because a taught perceiver must distinguish restraining worship from sound restraint. The later scene corrected any equation of teaching with mere information uptake.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_921aaf7c24bb9357cd3a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:5:1\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\"]],[\"96:5:5\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sonraki soru, öğretimin yalnızca bilgi edinmeye değil, Allah'ın gördüğünü bilerek ilişkisel sorumluluk taşımaya yöneldiğini gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b713505b02fd4046dd74","text":"1. **Acquired knowledge as evidence against self-sufficiency.** `ع ل م:B001` covers teaching, learning, and a thing becoming clear. The same root frames the human with causative `علّم` and negated simple `لم يعلم`; 96:7 then says that he sees himself as independent. The form contrast assigns knowledge’s arrival to teaching and its prior absence to the learner. The packet supplies that asymmetry; I infer that received knowledge is evidence of continuing dependence. The line therefore undercuts the later self-sufficient self-image from inside the human’s own knowing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d63a6f9824733b903bf7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:5:1\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\"]],[\"96:5:5\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bilginin öğretimle gelmesi ve önceden bulunmaması, insanın sonraki yeterlilik iddiasını kendi bilmesinin içinden çürütür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ed23f6148efe29390112","text":"2. **Teaching as making discriminable signs.** `ع ل م:B002` supplies the distinguishing mark that points to something. The preceding `بالقلم` gives a mark-making instrument, while 96:7 and 96:9 turn to seeing and evaluative attention. The branch’s role is to make teaching a process of marking differences so that an unknown can be recognized. I infer that the pen’s durable distinctions mediate the passage from `لم يعلم` to knowledge. The ayah reads as the production of discernibility, not simply the transfer of propositions.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
